# Reference parallel C — source reading and selected live states

- Date: 2026-10-07. Scope: Component Gallery, Uiverse, 3dicons, Refero.
- This is research evidence, not implementation, adoption, release, or exhaustive interaction QA.
- Existing captures were preserved. Only this report, its own reading index, and three proof PNGs were added. No shared source/ledger, package, lock, dist, Git, remote CI, or native runtime was changed.
- Reading means actually reading captured text; HTTP 200, inventory, and screenshots do not count as reading or interaction completion. Captured sources may differ from the current site.

## Refero: why extracted tokens cannot be adopted unchanged

[Wise style detail](https://styles.refero.design/style/367c0c6e-73a7-441c-a8ff-91d139ac60dc) places #9fe870 lime in conflicting roles. Its color-role prose/export says “Do not promote it to the primary CTA color”; its Guidelines recommend lime for primary-action fills and active controls. The description and segmented-control prose also use lime for active states. The page’s no-drop-shadow guidance conflicts with its Elevation recipes, including 10px/32px and 40px/40px card shadows. The observation is an inconsistency within Refero’s generated recommendations, not a finding about the original Wise product.

[Cursor style detail](https://styles.refero.design/style/4e3b4717-84c8-4599-baaf-a343c3d619b6) repeatedly exports invalid five-digit #26251 in prose/CSS examples while the palette’s Ink value is #26251e. Guidance about avoiding pill treatment conflicts with pill/button descriptions. Do not mechanically turn these exports into registered presets. Each role requires validation against HJM semantic intent and real rendered states.

Both pages distinguish normalized measurement, interpreted roles, and reconstructed HTML from original source components. Their image previews and component reconstructions provide visual direction; they do not establish original keyboard, responsive, loading, or business behavior. Original Wise/Cursor sites were not exercised here. Wise DesignTokens initial section and DESIGN.md major sections were read; remaining JSON and other export tabs remain pending. Cursor major DESIGN.md sections were read; the full quick-start/export set remains pending.

The 52 general pages consistently put product purpose and actual content first. Useful shared additions are usage guidance for display/reading/technical typography roles, section density/rhythm, screenshot crop/frame/mobile alternatives, and how a composition selects quiet versus operational presentation. Product requirements still own transaction rules, commercial prices, data, permissions, and state transitions. A proposed DESIGN.md specification and vendor-history assertions were read as page claims, not independently verified standards.

## 3dicons: selected live interaction chain

[Explore](https://3dicons.co/explore): clay + front radio state selected; visible image currentSrc paths and complete images matched front/clay. Typing Notebook alone did not update results; Enter returned one result. [Notebook detail](https://3dicons.co/icons/628100-notebook?color=clay) opened as a Dialog over Explore. Gradient + iso changed the real image to the 400px gradient isometric asset after an initial loading placeholder. The placeholder was transient, not a persistent defect.

Download opened a menu for current icon, angle bundles, .fbx, .blend, and all icons; no file was downloaded. Pressing Escape on the download trigger closed both the menu and the detail dialog, restoring the prior Notebook search with clay/front. This is observed nested Escape propagation; its code cause was not isolated. Popular sort was selected with one result, so ordering correctness was not demonstrated. Searching `no-such-icon-qa` with Enter displayed No icon to display and preserved clay/front.

Absorb through existing Asset/Image, Dialog/Menu, SearchField/EmptyState, and finite collection composition. A theme can select a material/angle bundle while products own asset meaning and license mapping. Decorative 3D illustrations should not replace functional Icon semantics or official provider assets. Preserve a loading fallback and image layout stability.

![3dicons no-result state](assets/parallel-c-3dicons-no-results.png)

Proof PNG SHA-256: `1d9338bbe3f0882df5cae6c62c345ddcf9df1e29714f39a7b947d13a2af6ffe1`.

## Component Gallery: one linked contract read, not complete original behavior

[Carousel page](https://component.gallery/components/carousel) has 22 examples. React filter showed 10, clear filters restored 22, name sort persisted. Gallery global search accepted Carousel by ArrowDown/Enter. The theme menu used System/Light/Dark menuitemradio; Dark changed the site canvas while static example images retained their original appearance. System was restored.

[Cedar Filmstrip](https://cedar.rei.com/components/filmstrip) full body was read: purpose, applicability, anatomy, interaction, content, accessibility responsibility, adapter/model/frame implementation, events, API. It uses five parts, finite related items, partially visible frames to communicate overflow, and owner-provided data adapters. Whole-frame navigation and nested actions need distinct focus handling. The page says Storybook documentation is not available for this component; no working Filmstrip demo was exercised.

Current HJM web Carousel is a single active finite panel; inactive panels are hidden/inert. Cedar’s multiple-visible scroll model is therefore a distinct collection presentation contract, not a safe cosmetic theme switch. A finite filmstrip/peek composition is a candidate for an explicit presentation capability with focus, scroll, and announcement contracts. It should not silently change Carousel semantics.

## Uiverse: reuse a treatment, retain the Button contract

[3D-buttons category](https://uiverse.io/ui/3d-buttons) and [thin-owl-11](https://uiverse.io/njesenberger/thin-owl-11) were read. The post has a native button plus top/bottom/base decorative layers. CSS uses inset shading, radial gradients, 200ms transitions, and a 6px active translation. Space/focus appearance was observed; an application action result or a held pressed frame was not verified. The inspected CSS has no explicit disabled/loading or prefers-reduced-motion handling; this is a source observation, not complete accessibility QA.

The useful addition is an optional press-feedback treatment on the existing HJM Button engine, with disabled/pending, keyboard, reduced motion and native equivalents preserved. Do not add a second functional Button simply to copy this skin. HJM profiles currently expose contentTransition and selectionMotion; press feedback can be assessed as a separate capability. The post’s MIT notice was read; substantial copied code would require its attribution. No code was copied or installed.

## Outcome and follow-up

This pass reduces Refero’s captured general-body pending denominator from 52 to zero, without marking visual/behavior completeness. The owned JSON contains one URL/hash/finding for every completed general page. Fixed sorted style detail reading is next; any further completion must record an actually read body, not a keyword match. Whole-site completion still requires remaining bodies and real UI/interaction coverage. Research judgments above are proposals; source implementation changes remain parent-owned.

## Ordered style-body reading checkpoint

Full captured text, including canonical DESIGN.md, generated CSS/Tailwind quick starts and similar-card labels, was read for the first two hundred and thirty lexically sorted style URLs. This is 230/1,342 style bodies; complete visual and behavioral review of these two hundred and thirty remains pending. The owned index records each hash and reading judgment.

- [pampam.city](https://styles.refero.design/style/001480cb-05f4-4802-be39-84b942169481): paper canvas, display serif and tilted browser-frame composition. Periwinkle role prohibits primary CTA while Guidelines and reconstructed Button prescribe it; the agent guide also says no distinct CTA. Nineties fallback is sans in quick-start CSS despite a serif substitute recommendation. OpenType features are described but absent from quick-start recipes. This strengthens the case for role validation rather than raw preset imports.

Monochrome recommendations must not suppress semantic error/status accessibility.

## Live provenance mismatch: labelled TeePublic

The fourteenth sorted [Refero detail](https://styles.refero.design/style/0231caf5-0347-4f2d-ba20-5bab8fcaf2ce) is titled TeePublic throughout its description and DESIGN.md, but the origin link is https://unfurproject.com. The current live page reproduces that title/link mismatch. Its visible preview contains unrelated lottery promotion imagery; this is not verified evidence of the original TeePublic brand design. No original-domain browsing, registration, transaction or external mutation was performed. This is an observed provenance problem within the reference record, not a claim about the cause or current state of TeePublic itself.

Exclude the record from official-brand implementation evidence until its origin/capture/identity are reconciled. Its body also recommends no large violet fills while describing full-width violet promo/trust bands, and its red role differs from urgent-state recommendations. Those source recommendations were read, but should not enter a preset without validation.

Every-button 100px radius conflicts with the 110px header CTA and 9999px guide; geometry must resolve with responsive size rather than literal universal radii. Bone #f1efdf canvas differs from quilt bone #efefef (Ash Gray), preserve distinct surfaces. Marquee motion/pause, photo crop, hero dynamic viewport, nav dropdown/language, forms and mobile quilt collapse remain unverified; imagery is not evidence of a procedural paper/forest texture.

107. [Swap design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/15d10d6c-1844-46c6-ae69-c99a56e6ad41) — Mint editorial greenhouse: text-driven hero, light serif display with lime-to-citrus gradient clipped to glyphs, pill paired actions, white soft-shadow cards and five-column logo grid. Actual logo rights/content remain product-owned. Gradient text and lime focus border need semantic contrast separate from black-on-lime CTA: independent opaque-sRGB checks of the three prescribed gradient stops on Mint Wash #f2ffe3 are 1.216/1.176/1.078:1, below the ordinary large-text threshold; this is a reconstruction color-pair calculation, not original rendering proof. A low-contrast decorative gradient must not replace readable heading content. Custom-serif placeholder has a serif fallback while detected secondaryFont exports sans; resolve actual face mapping/weight support instead of copying unresolved aliases. Gradient-only-display minimum 72px conflicts with no-below-48 rule and 48–72 section-headline recipe. CSS has gradient while Tailwind omits it; ss01 feature is prose-only, and 80-120px/8-16px custom-property ranges are not directly usable CSS lengths. Guideline weight100 hero/features differs from300 section recipes; make typography roles explicit. Forms/focus, nav, logo-grid collapse, responsive long headings and reduced-motion remain unverified.

## Experiment registration candidates — pending, C282

User requested registration after research (2026-10-07). The following is the manual mapping of all candidate classes found in the 52 general + 230 detail readings and selected Component Gallery/Uiverse/3dicons review. It is a proposed experiment backlog; no experiment was registered here. Each own-index page carries candidate IDs, and each catalog entry preserves every assigned source URL. The same treatment found on many pages has one candidate. Source errors become validation fixtures, not copied product tokens. Further unread pages may add candidates.

The proposed path is a Storybook hierarchy string, not a created file. Existing API references are verified read-only against current source; public additions and actual registration belong to root. Paths were corrected against [Storybook navigation §1.1–1.3](../STORYBOOK_NAVIGATION.md): exactly four segments, existing fixed category vocabulary, unique Korean item names of at most 13 characters (limit 16). Current literal story titles were read for name collisions; dynamic titles and actual registration must still pass the normal registration checks. Collapsible maps to data display per the contract catalog. S06/G01 are comparison compositions, not complete screen flows. No new category was added.

Excluded as product defaults: disputed TeePublic identity/lottery preview; original brand font/logo/provider assets without ownership or licensing; product prices, trades, subscriptions, permissions, flight/LED/delivery/live data; semantic danger/success inferred from brand pigments; raw invalid hex/unitless physical leading/prose CSS ranges; one-brand bans on light/dark mode, readable typography, focus indicators, current-state semantics or required actions; forced decorative clipping/animation; photography described as paper/linen/stone/glass treated as a procedural material without proof. These exclusions retain the related usable presentation candidates above. Original product state matrices remain pending.

108. [Wemakethings design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/15d57573-513b-49aa-91c7-1b7f87bb1a55) — Brutalist ink-on-white broadsheet: huge stroke-only BASEBLOOM type is explicitly decorative scaffolding behind readable Maison headings, with full-height vertical brand-name columns and hairline separators. Source explicitly says the 864px backdrop never carries readable information, so preserve decorative/aria-hidden/pointer-free separation and responsive stroke handling rather than duplicating semantic headings. A rotated/stacked brand wall needs a readable list alternative and logical link/tab order; do not infer working scroll behavior from its layout prose. No-padding column recipe conflicts with 20px prompt, and body16/1.33 role is absent as a named body scale while caption16 and body-lg18/1.2 are exported. 80-120px section gap is a prose range. Arrow-as-affordance and strict monochrome/no-photos rules are local presentation recommendations, not permission to remove semantic labels, status cues, focus, required image content or RTL direction. Original brand-list scrolling, navigation/current underline, wrapping and font asset/outline loading are unverified.

109. [Contra design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/1608cf19-b249-46d4-bd37-b4c6a7fc4b56) — Creative-marketplace portfolio wall: mode segment→hero/search→four-card topics→section-header/project mosaics, with two-column featured inverted modules. Category colors rotate as a product taxonomy deck and photo cards use a prescribed 40% scrim; neither defines semantic status or proves every crop readable. Search includes an embedded action, filter chips have optional close controls, and stacked-avatar/count metadata should preserve individual semantics and large-text layout. GT Standard L below19 ban conflicts with its 8/12px detected roles; hero-only display rule conflicts featured-brand23–58px recipe. Six-only-radius rule conflicts raw63/100px exports and variable16–40px tag values. The two-layer-charcoal-only shadow direction differs inset active-tab and black sticky-nav elevation; preserve scopes. GT Standard L suggested substitute identities are unverified, while CSS exports sans fallback, so resolve owned faces rather than transplant brand files. Hero gradient is absent as a full Tailwind gradient export. Hiring mode, search submit, filters/close, grids, profile navigation/auth, scroll and responsive behavior remain unverified in original Contra; no marketplace claims or counts are validated.

110. [Skillshare design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/162e9ba9-7487-4b57-aee8-0bbeadcc586d) — Mixed black/white poster-course presentation: headline/registration split hero, photo category rail, instructor portraits with bottom gradient scrim, surface-raised dark stats and checklist. Source explicitly separates brand green action from safety; Google/Facebook logo colors are provider-owned despite being placed under Semantic in Refero. Do not register those pigments as HJM semantic tokens or reconstruct provider logos; preserve HJM AuthProviderButton assets/provider guidance and AuthScreenLayout centered primary group/footer consent/pending contracts. The inline 380px registration half-panel is reference marketing layout, not authority to replace the portfolio login standard or enable a new provider. Warning Amber is used ratings/achievement, not evidence of warning state. Body no-below1.4 rule applies only13–18px, so20px subheading1.3 is not itself a contradiction; preview16px/1 and Times fallback1 still require role resolution. Times fallback-only identification is a source claim and its CSS sans fallback differs from Georgia substitute. All4px card rule has explicit asymmetric4/8 portrait and8stat exceptions; 0.1em×11px=1.1px caption tracking is coherent. Tiny labels, 5px auth/button padding, crop scrim, category rail, sticky search, signup/auth/terms/loading/error and responsive states require original checks; no authentication was performed.

111. [Rainbow design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/1680693c-aed8-47e8-917a-04eb89497b09) — Candy rounded presentation: glossy rainbow 3D assets, large rounded type, pastel multi-stop full-bleed atmosphere, inset-highlight device frames and five phone-feature cards. Brand rainbow tile beside every CTA is an owned asset slot, not a generic functional icon or requirement for all apps. Network-switch toast uses red Optimism identity, not danger; the floating notification may be a screenshot/reconstruction and was not exercised. Palette prohibits tangerine/pink CTA promotion while canonical/buttons prescribe them and AgentGuide says none; white nav button is another scope exception. Independent opaque-sRGB white-on-tangerine/pink source-pair ratios are 2.363/2.901:1 for16px700 action labels, below ordinary small-text contrast; no original rendered audit claimed. Type tracking -0.03em at100px should be -3px but exported -0.03px, with analogous smaller roles; previews/export regular400 versus600–900 guidance, and body500 versus minimum600 require actual weight mapping. Forty–50px all-interactive radius conflicts16px toast/9999px nav, no-outer-shadow guidance conflicts toast drop shadow, and no-contained-gradient rule later permits large rounded shapes. CSS full gradients are absent from Tailwind. No procedural texture or verified wallet/network/download/Toast behavior is established; original responsive, focus, paused motion and assets remain pending.

112. [Intra design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/16a8de02-a4c6-4077-9d3a-ef6b5c10db12) — White gallery cafe: single400 type hierarchy, giant eye brand asset, three-column eye/text/coffee-card composition, hairline framed packaging and documentary photos with occasional ink inverse panel. Package magenta/lime/terracotta are expressly image content, not new UI accent or status tokens. The oval eye/pupil is brand artwork; universal0px component corners must not flatten intrinsic rounded asset geometry. 95px-only/no-wrap wordmark and1px vertical outlined-action padding cannot override responsive reflow, scaling and hit targets. Photo block says full-bleed or column-width while imagery says always-contained78px gutters, and hero eye/no-headline versus wordmark hero prompt are reconstruction alternatives not original observations. Fixed50/78px gutters require actual mobile validation. PureBlack role phrase absolute-zero-contrast does not describe its contrast against white; use semantic contrast checks. Minimal nav is explicitly likely, not proof of a working nav map. Meta hours/contact rows may reuse label/value collections; opening hours, venue inventory and buy-in-store action are product content, no online checkout was established. Original navigation, card/link focus, image crops, fonts and responsive states remain pending.

113. [Outsource Consultants design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/16be276a-d8ce-484e-8f7a-cbbb09f717f7) — Bone/indigo architectural broadsheet: large flush-left/split headings, rotated right-margin annotation, mono metadata and hairline service accordion with plus→cross decoration. Reuse disclosure semantics for expanded panels; the cross glyph is not evidence of a separate close transaction or an actual keyboard/animation state. Mono10px descriptions with -.05em tracking and physical low-opacity divider need actual legibility/focus/zoom checks; caption -.5px conversion is coherent. Header/nav10px tracking+.02 differs general at10px-.05 instruction, and preview160px500 differs canonical400. Layout says no centered max-width while token/export1200px remains, and no padded cards differs20px card-padding token; resolve by scope. Mandatory no-wrap160px headings cannot block responsive content. Paper role forbids primary CTA while Menu is white neutral-fill; action intent must remain even without chromatic CTA. Refero origin is https://oci.madebybuzzworthy.com, an agency subdomain rather than a verified final canonical consultant URL; preserve provenance and do not claim production original behavior. No real accordion expand/collapse, full menu, focus/escape, mobile layout or contact flow was exercised.

114. [TWOTWO design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/170a690b-7424-4c2e-9d08-975725cf9261) — Monochrome sports catalog with single voltage-lime action, large lime headline over raw hero photo, 3:4 product cards, 3-column→1-column collection and quiet technical callout. Product colors remain photography; a lime swatch tag is not evidence of a selected variant or actual product-color mapping. Radius-as-interactive-vs-static claim cannot provide semantic clickability or replace accessible labels/focus; 50px controls and16px images are role treatments, while exported8px radius contradicts two-only rule. Whyte inktrap monospace-feeling does not prove monospace font identity, and Whyte display size summary13–38 conflicts prose72/32; resolve owned faces/roles instead of duplicate alias registry. Correct72px×-.02em=-1.44px display tracking is present, but general nav+.04em is absent from exported caption0. Source2045 border count and18.2:1 claim are source measurements, not independently verified original census. Source functional-only lime conflicts decorative-highlight palette phrase and centered technical paragraph conflicts no-centered-body rule; use scoped roles. No-gradient-overlay photo headline requires actual crop contrast; fixed560px hero and40px utility targets need device/large-text review. Search/currency/cart/variant/focus/mobile and transactions remain unverified.

115. [Hardworkclub design system | Refero Styles](https://styles.refero.design/style/1775bf6a-afdd-48b1-8435-b92ec585c674) — Black-box cinematic gallery: one cobalt circular menu control, centered serif wordmark, full-bleed film/still, left editorial intro and paired arch-top/rectangular work cards. Cobalt button has no visible text; retain actual accessible name and menu expanded/focus/escape semantics, not color-only affordance. Arch top24px+ and8px bottom differ from a uniform radius token; true silhouette depends on image size/clip geometry and needs actual visual proof. Maligna serif recommendations conflict with quick-start sans fallback; em-to-px tracking61×-.03=-1.83px and18×-.03=-.54px is coherent. Preview18px leading.78 versus canonical body1.5 needs role resolution. No z-axis/elevation prose cannot remove implementation stacking needed for menu/overlap; distinguish visual shadow from layer order. 1440/999999px radii and96-160px gap are measurements/prose, not new global scales. White words over underexposed imagery need per-frame contrast; underexposure is not an accessibility proof. Two-up-pair and black-only brand rules cannot suppress product required content/modes. C222 checkpoint: 222/1,394 captured bodies read = 52 general + 170 style details; 1,172 style details remain. All 41 candidate classes remain proposed-not-registered. Phantom’s intentionally low-contrast helper text is not an accessibility exemption. Jeton’s collective-wisdom/brain recipe has unresolved provenance and is excluded from product defaults pending original-source comparison.

Root-owned 3dicons follow-up is separate from this captured-body reading count: Tick five loaded variants (gradient/front selected only), Ghost color/front and clay/dynamic with a 390px dark/reduced-motion view, and two Threads assets with front/dynamic. Keyboard download-menu Escape focus restoration and a missing radio-group label target were checked by root. Asset profile-radius inheritance was repaired and checked separately by root; these results do not complete every original icon combination or Native device review.

## Captured detail body batch 171–190 — C242

Twenty full captured bodies were manually read in lexical URL order, including source metadata, canonical DESIGN.md, generated CSS/Tailwind and similar labels. Exact repeated blocks in Midjourney were shown once with original-line references; no unique source content was omitted. Source SHA refers to raw captured HTML, not interpreted CSS. Original pixels, source implementation and complete state matrices remain pending.

Static opaque sRGB recipes: MotherDuck White/Sky=1.938:1 versus Charcoal/Sky=6.051:1; Forner Pebble/Bone=1.366:1; Mural808080/White=3.949:1; Recess Slate/Periwinkle=1.769:1; BaseHub Bone/Orange=2.558:1 versus303030/Orange=4.649:1; Huddle Honey/White=1.826:1. Shelby Olive/Linen=9.166:1 and Sage/Linen=3.698:1 agree with the source rounded9.2/3.7 claim; they do not prove10px legibility. No calculation is original rendered QA.
